{"forms":[{"form":"hypocrisies","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"political hypocrisy","type":"example"},{"text":"He condemned the hypocrisy of politicians who broke their own laws.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The contrivance of a false appearance of virtue or goodness, while concealing real character or inclinations, especially with respect to religious and moral beliefs; hence in general sense, dissimulation, pretence, sham."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"rank hypocrisy","type":"example"},{"text":"charge of hypocrisy","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The claim or pretense of having beliefs, standards, qualities, behaviours, virtues, motivations, etc. which one does not really have."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["The practice of engaging in the same behaviour or activity for which one criticises another; moral self-contradiction whereby the behavior of one or more people belies their own claimed or implied possession of certain beliefs, standards or virtues."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["An instance of hypocrisy."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/hɪˈpɒkɹəsi/","tags":["UK"]},{"ipa":"/hɪˈpɑkɹəsi/","tags":["US"]}],"synonyms":[{"word":"hypocriticality"},{"word":"hypocriticalness"}],"upstream_line":268096,"upstream_offset":1342777250,"upstream_record_sha256":"ef9f0594d5a03b24e4b5bc0da191b5e398547dbbf52c8d5aa53b039bff94355b","word":"hypocrisy"}
